tmp_path = WindowsPath('<home>/AppData/Local/Temp/pytest-of-user/pytest-8/test_admission_reads_only_decl0')

    @pytest.fixture
    def capsule(tmp_path):
        original, static, semantics = (tmp_path / name for name in ("original", "static", "semantics"))
        fixture = original / "source/fixture"
        fixture.mkdir(parents=True)
        (original / "selected-24").mkdir()
        static.mkdir()
        semantics.mkdir()
        verification_sources = tmp_path / "verification-sources"
        verification_sources.mkdir()
        script = verification_sources / "native_modality_static_audit.py"
        script.write_text("# static arithmetic producer\n")
        archive, provenance = {}, {"panels": {}}
        for index, (name, count) in enumerate(reference.PANELS.items()):
            x = np.zeros((count, 650))
            x[:, 0] = np.arange(count) + index / 10
            y = np.arange(count, dtype=np.int64)
            archive[name + "_inputs"], archive[name + "_labels"] = x, y
            provenance["panels"][name] = [
                {"input_sha256": reference.sha_bytes(row), "label": int(label)}
                for row, label in zip(x, y, strict=True)
            ]
        # Loading this member with allow_pickle=False raises. Admission must leave it sealed.
        archive["heldout_inputs"] = np.array([object()], dtype=object)
        np.savez_compressed(fixture / "school.npz", **archive)
        reference.write(fixture / "provenance.json", provenance)
        reference.write(
            original / "protocol.json",
            {"fixture": {"school.npz": reference.sha(fixture / "school.npz")}},
        )
        reference.write(original / "summary.json", {"scope": "synthetic admission fixture"})
        (original / "source/extract.py").write_text("# fixed body codec\n")
        for name in ("initial", "final"):
            (original / f"selected-24/{name}.npz").write_bytes(name.encode())
        for name in ("eye.py", "collect.py"):
            (semantics / name).write_text("# current semantic reference\n")
        np.savez_compressed(static / "direct-current-arrays.npz", initial_sensory_s=np.zeros((61, 650)))
        static_job = {
            "script_sha256": reference.sha(script),
            "source_data_pins": {
                str(path.relative_to(original)): reference.sha(path)
                for path in (
                    original / "protocol.json",
                    original / "summary.json",
                    fixture / "school.npz",
                    fixture / "provenance.json",
                    original / "selected-24/initial.npz",
                    original / "selected-24/final.npz",
                )
            },
        }
        reference.write(static / "job-protocol.json", static_job)
        reference.write(
            static / "result.json",
            {
                "verified": True,
                "heldout_decoded": False,
                "job_protocol_sha256": reference.sha(static / "job-protocol.json"),
                "raw_arrays_sha256": reference.sha(static / "direct-current-arrays.npz"),
            },
        )
        root = tmp_path / "prepared"

root = WindowsPath('<home>/AppData/Local/Temp/pytest-of-user/pytest-8/test_admission_reads_only_decl0/prepared')
protocol = {'schema': 'cadence-native-information-reference-v1', 'panels': {'school': 24, 'independent_train': 18, 'development':...40], 'body': [640, 650]}, 'feature_modes': ['raw_full', 'balanced_full', 'scene', 'motion', 'fovea', 'body', ...], ...}
runtime = False

    def guard(root, protocol, *, runtime=True):
        if any(protocol.get(key) != value for key, value in FIXED.items()):
            raise ValueError("frozen native reference scope/config/bounds changed")
        for name, expected in protocol["sources"].items():
            if sha(root / "source" / name) != expected:
                raise ValueError("frozen reference source/data changed: " + name)
        if sha(Path(__file__)) != protocol["sources"]["native_information_controls.py"]:
            raise ValueError("executed reference helper differs")
        if protocol["runtime"] != {
            "numpy": np.__version__,
            "interpreter_sha256": sha(Path(sys.executable)),
        }:
            raise ValueError("reference runtime differs")
        if runtime and any(os.environ.get(key) != value for key, value in THREADS.items()):
            raise ValueError("reference thread settings differ")
        job = json.loads((root / "job-protocol.json").read_text())
        if job["protocol_sha256"] != sha(root / "protocol.json"):
            raise ValueError("reference reviewed job binding differs")
        # Static feature arrays are bound to the original24-only graph/checkpoints,
        # and contain only61 admitted rows in school/TRAIN/development order.
        static_job = json.loads((root / "source/static-job.json").read_text())
        static_result = json.loads((root / "source/static-result.json").read_text())
        original = json.loads((root / "source/original-protocol.json").read_text())
        if (
            not static_result["verified"]
            or static_result["heldout_decoded"]
            or static_result["job_protocol_sha256"] != sha(root / "source/static-job.json")
            or static_result["raw_arrays_sha256"] != sha(root / "source/direct-current-arrays.npz")
            or static_job["source_data_pins"]["protocol.json"]
            != sha(root / "source/original-protocol.json")
            or static_job["source_data_pins"]["summary.json"]
            != sha(root / "source/original-summary.json")
>           or static_job["source_data_pins"]["source/fixture/school.npz"]

    def guard(root, protocol, *, runtime=True):
        if any(protocol.get(key) != value for key, value in FIXED.items()):
            raise ValueError("frozen native reference scope/config/bounds changed")
        for name, expected in protocol["sources"].items():
            if sha(root / "source" / name) != expected:
                raise ValueError("frozen reference source/data changed: " + name)
        if sha(Path(__file__)) != protocol["sources"]["native_information_controls.py"]:
            raise ValueError("executed reference helper differs")
        if protocol["runtime"] != {
            "numpy": np.__version__,
            "interpreter_sha256": sha(Path(sys.executable)),
        }:
            raise ValueError("reference runtime differs")
        if runtime and any(os.environ.get(key) != value for key, value in THREADS.items()):
            raise ValueError("reference thread settings differ")
        job = json.loads((root / "job-protocol.json").read_text())
        if job["protocol_sha256"] != sha(root / "protocol.json"):
            raise ValueError("reference reviewed job binding differs")
        # Static feature arrays are bound to the original24-only graph/checkpoints,
        # and contain only61 admitted rows in school/TRAIN/development order.
        static_job = json.loads((root / "source/static-job.json").read_text())
        static_result = json.loads((root / "source/static-result.json").read_text())
        original = json.loads((root / "source/original-protocol.json").read_text())
        if (
            not static_result["verified"]
            or static_result["heldout_decoded"]
            or static_result["job_protocol_sha256"] != sha(root / "source/static-job.json")
            or static_result["raw_arrays_sha256"] != sha(root / "source/direct-current-arrays.npz")
            or static_job["source_data_pins"]["protocol.json"]

tmp_path = WindowsPath('<home>/AppData/Local/Temp/pytest-of-user/pytest-8/test_bounds_roles_and_original3')

    @pytest.fixture
    def capsule(tmp_path):
        original, static, semantics = (tmp_path / name for name in ("original", "static", "semantics"))
        fixture = original / "source/fixture"
        fixture.mkdir(parents=True)
        (original / "selected-24").mkdir()
        static.mkdir()
        semantics.mkdir()
        verification_sources = tmp_path / "verification-sources"
        verification_sources.mkdir()
        script = verification_sources / "native_modality_static_audit.py"
        script.write_text("# static arithmetic producer\n")
        archive, provenance = {}, {"panels": {}}
        for index, (name, count) in enumerate(reference.PANELS.items()):
            x = np.zeros((count, 650))
            x[:, 0] = np.arange(count) + index / 10
            y = np.arange(count, dtype=np.int64)
            archive[name + "_inputs"], archive[name + "_labels"] = x, y
            provenance["panels"][name] = [
                {"input_sha256": reference.sha_bytes(row), "label": int(label)}
                for row, label in zip(x, y, strict=True)
            ]
        # Loading this member with allow_pickle=False raises. Admission must leave it sealed.
        archive["heldout_inputs"] = np.array([object()], dtype=object)
        np.savez_compressed(fixture / "school.npz", **archive)
        reference.write(fixture / "provenance.json", provenance)
        reference.write(
            original / "protocol.json",
            {"fixture": {"school.npz": reference.sha(fixture / "school.npz")}},
        )
        reference.write(original / "summary.json", {"scope": "synthetic admission fixture"})
        (original / "source/extract.py").write_text("# fixed body codec\n")
        for name in ("initial", "final"):
            (original / f"selected-24/{name}.npz").write_bytes(name.encode())
        for name in ("eye.py", "collect.py"):
            (semantics / name).write_text("# current semantic reference\n")
        np.savez_compressed(static / "direct-current-arrays.npz", initial_sensory_s=np.zeros((61, 650)))
        static_job = {
            "script_sha256": reference.sha(script),
            "source_data_pins": {
                str(path.relative_to(original)): reference.sha(path)
                for path in (
                    original / "protocol.json",
                    original / "summary.json",
                    fixture / "school.npz",
                    fixture / "provenance.json",
                    original / "selected-24/initial.npz",
                    original / "selected-24/final.npz",
                )
            },
        }
        reference.write(static / "job-protocol.json", static_job)
        reference.write(
            static / "result.json",
            {
                "verified": True,
                "heldout_decoded": False,
                "job_protocol_sha256": reference.sha(static / "job-protocol.json"),
                "raw_arrays_sha256": reference.sha(static / "direct-current-arrays.npz"),
            },
        )
        root = tmp_path / "prepared"

receipt = Receipt(kind='cadence-attention-heads-continuation/1', body={'combined_relevance': {'budget_scope': 'additional_releva...ea6288e21d84fd484a71144af2c0ef5bd51eaf2a6'}, digest='12c384dae5992e3675baf2d74ec38d680fc90ada107ae99598036d146d065a40')

    def _resolved_sources(receipt: Receipt) -> list[tuple[str, Path]]:
        """Resolve retained bytes without treating them as the currently installed model."""
        sources = []
        for entry in receipt.source["files"]:
            relative = PurePosixPath(entry["path"])
            digest = entry["sha256"]
            if (relative.is_absolute() or ".." in relative.parts
                    or not isinstance(digest, str) or len(digest) != 64
                    or any(c not in "0123456789abcdef" for c in digest)):
                raise ValueError("invalid source manifest path or digest")
            snapshot = HERE / "results" / "sources" / (digest + ".txt")
            source = snapshot if snapshot.exists() else ROOT / relative
            try:
                actual = hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            except OSError as error:
                raise ValueError(f"historical source bytes unavailable: {relative}") from error
            if actual != digest:
>               raise ValueError(f"historical source digest differs: {relative}")

    def _resolved_sources(receipt: Receipt) -> list[tuple[str, Path]]:
        """Resolve retained bytes without treating them as the currently installed model."""
        sources = []
        for entry in receipt.source["files"]:
            relative = PurePosixPath(entry["path"])
            digest = entry["sha256"]
            if (relative.is_absolute() or ".." in relative.parts
                    or not isinstance(digest, str) or len(digest) != 64
                    or any(c not in "0123456789abcdef" for c in digest)):
                raise ValueError("invalid source manifest path or digest")
            snapshot = HERE / "results" / "sources" / (digest + ".txt")
            source = snapshot if snapshot.exists() else ROOT / relative
            try:
                actual = hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            except OSError as error:
                raise ValueError(f"historical source bytes unavailable: {relative}") from error
            if actual != digest:
>               raise ValueError(f"historical source digest differs: {relative}")

    def test_local_documentation_links_resolve():
        problems = []
        for page in [
            ROOT / "README.md",
            ROOT / "MANIFESTO.md",
            ROOT / "CONTRIBUTING.md",
            ROOT / "AGENTS.md",
            *sorted((ROOT / "docs").rglob("*.md")),
        ]:
            for target in re.findall(r"\[[^\]\n]*\]\(([^\s)]+)\)", page.read_text()):
                if re.match(r"[a-z]+:", target):
                    continue
                path, _, anchor = unquote(target).partition("#")
                destination = (page.parent / path).resolve() if path else page
                if not destination.exists():
                    problems.append(f"{page.name}: missing {target}")
                elif anchor and destination.suffix == ".md":
>                   content = destination.read_text()
                              ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

            root / name for name in (f"seed-{seed}", f"seed-{seed}-old4", f"seed-{seed}-mixed")
            if (root / name).is_dir()
        ]
        hashes = {
            str(p.relative_to(root)): harness.sha256(p)
            for folder in paths
            for p in sorted(folder.rglob("*"))
            if p.is_file()
        }
        archive = root / f"seed-{seed}.tar.gz"
        with tarfile.open(archive, "w:gz", compresslevel=9) as bundle:
            for folder in paths:
                bundle.add(folder, arcname=folder.name)
        observed = {}
        with tarfile.open(archive, "r:gz") as bundle:
            for member in bundle.getmembers():
                if member.isfile():
                    observed[member.name] = hashlib.sha256(
                        bundle.extractfile(member).read()
                    ).hexdigest()
        if observed != hashes:
>           raise AssertionError("outer archive does not preserve every original member byte")

    def _resolved_sources(receipt: Receipt) -> list[tuple[str, Path]]:
        """Resolve retained bytes without treating them as the currently installed model."""
        sources = []
        for entry in receipt.source["files"]:
            relative = PurePosixPath(entry["path"])
            digest = entry["sha256"]
            if (relative.is_absolute() or ".." in relative.parts
                    or not isinstance(digest, str) or len(digest) != 64
                    or any(c not in "0123456789abcdef" for c in digest)):
                raise ValueError("invalid source manifest path or digest")
            snapshot = HERE / "results" / "sources" / (digest + ".txt")
            source = snapshot if snapshot.exists() else ROOT / relative
            try:
                actual = hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            except OSError as error:
                raise ValueError(f"historical source bytes unavailable: {relative}") from error
            if actual != digest:
>               raise ValueError(f"historical source digest differs: {relative}")
E               ValueError: historical source digest differs: benchmarks/attention/diagnose.py

prior = WindowsPath('P:/projects/cadence/benchmarks/attention/results/heads-capacity-corrected.json')

    def verify_prior(prior: Path) -> Receipt:
        """A historical failure is checked against ITS protocol and source snapshots."""
        receipt = Receipt.read(prior)
        prior_path = protocol_path(receipt.body.get("protocol_file", "heads-protocol.json"))
        prior_protocol = json.loads(prior_path.read_text())
        expected = sources(prior_path)
        if [entry["path"] for entry in receipt.source["files"]] != [name for name, _ in expected]:
            raise ValueError("prior source manifest paths differ")
        frozen = []
        for entry, (name, current) in zip(receipt.source["files"], expected, strict=True):
            digest = entry["sha256"]
            if (not isinstance(digest, str) or len(digest) != 64
                    or any(character not in "0123456789abcdef" for character in digest)):
                raise ValueError("invalid prior source digest")
            snapshot = HERE / "results" / "sources" / (digest + ".txt")
            frozen.append((name, snapshot if snapshot.exists() else current))
        ok, message = Receipt.verify(
            prior, sources=frozen, check=lambda body: check(body, protocol=prior_protocol))
        if (not ok or receipt.kind != "cadence-attention-heads-admission/1"
                or receipt.body["capacity_admitted"]):
>           raise ValueError("prior failed admission must verify: " + message)
E           ValueError: prior failed admission must verify: source manifest differs from the current code or data

    def _resolved_sources(receipt: Receipt) -> list[tuple[str, Path]]:
        """Resolve retained bytes without treating them as the currently installed model."""
        sources = []
        for entry in receipt.source["files"]:
            relative = PurePosixPath(entry["path"])
            digest = entry["sha256"]
            if (relative.is_absolute() or ".." in relative.parts
                    or not isinstance(digest, str) or len(digest) != 64
                    or any(c not in "0123456789abcdef" for c in digest)):
                raise ValueError("invalid source manifest path or digest")
            snapshot = HERE / "results" / "sources" / (digest + ".txt")
            source = snapshot if snapshot.exists() else ROOT / relative
            try:
                actual = hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            except OSError as error:
                raise ValueError(f"historical source bytes unavailable: {relative}") from error
            if actual != digest:
>               raise ValueError(f"historical source digest differs: {relative}")
E               ValueError: historical source digest differs: benchmarks/attention/heads_continuation.py

    def prior():
        ctx, spec = context(), declared()["prior"]
        path = ctx.runtime / "benchmarks/attention/results" / spec["file"]
        # Cache verification arithmetic only, after exact complete dependency hashes match.
        # No model, parameter, optimizer, memory or result-state cache is introduced.
        identity = (
            hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
            canonical_sha256(source_manifest(ctx.campaign.sources("h2"))),
            canonical_sha256(spec),
        )
        if ctx.prior_cache is not None and ctx.prior_cache[0] == identity:
            return Receipt.read(path)  # A caller cannot mutate the cached verified body.
        receipt = Receipt.read(path)
        valid, message = ctx.campaign.verify(path)
        if (
            not valid
            or receipt.kind != spec["kind"]
            or receipt.digest != spec["digest"]
            or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != spec["archive_sha256"]
            or receipt.body["h2_admitted"] is not False

            p["schema"] != "cadence-neutral-variation-two-binding-campaign/1"
            or p["query_scale"] != 1

    def test_synthetic_full_controller_capacity_h2_retention_and_schedule_verification(
        ctx, monkeypatch, tmp_path
    ):
        """Exercise real receipt/snapshot plumbing with explicitly fake neural boundaries."""
        from dataclasses import asdict
    
        from cadence.receipts import Receipt
    
        def phase_work(actions=1):
            work = ctx.sr.Work()
            work.actions, work.action_rows = actions, 8 * actions
            work.action_sweeps, work.action_row_sweeps = actions, 8 * actions
            work.action_residual_checks, work.action_row_residual_checks = actions, 8 * actions
            return work
    
        def fake_evaluate(brain, arm, initial, deadline, *, causal=False):
            del brain, arm, initial, deadline
            rows, readings = ctx.task.panel("all"), {}
            for condition in ctx.base.CONDITIONS if causal else ("intact",):
                expected = (
                    ctx.task.flipped(rows, int(condition == "flip_B"))
                    if condition.startswith("flip_")
                    else rows
                )
                answer = ctx.task.targets(expected)
                slot = {
                    "erase_0": 0,
                    "erase_1": 1,
                    "head_0": 0,
                    "head_1": 1,
                    "cloned_heads": 1,
                    "frozen_interface": 0,
                    "shuffled_interface": 1,
                }.get(condition)
                if slot is not None:
                    answer[:, slot] = 1 - answer[:, slot]
                weights = np.zeros((2, 8, 3))
                weights[0, np.arange(8), rows[:, 2]] = 0.9
                weights[1, np.arange(8), 1 - rows[:, 2]] = 0.9
                readings[condition] = ctx.base.reading(
                    answer, expected, phase_work(), "completed", attention=weights.tolist()
                )
            return {"prefix_work": asdict(phase_work(3)), "conditions": readings}
    
        def fake_train(brain, arm, schedule, deadline, *, offset=0):
            del arm, deadline
            brain.learner.updates += len(schedule)
            brain.learner.contrast_updates += len(schedule)
            return {
                "completed": True,
                "rounds": len(schedule),
                "wrong_counts": [0] * len(schedule),
                "stop": None,
                "work": asdict(ctx.sr.Work()),
                "global_round_offset": offset,
                "joint_schedule_sha256": subject.joint_schedule_digest(schedule, offset=offset),
                "completed_neutral_amplitudes": subject.neutral_amplitudes(
                    len(schedule), offset=offset
                ).tolist(),
            }
    
        def fixture_sources(stage):
            paths = [
                ("fixture/controller.py", Path(subject.__file__)),
                ("fixture/protocol.json", subject.PROTOCOL),
            ]
            if stage == "h2":
                paths.append(("fixture/new-capacity.json", subject._CAPACITY))
            return paths
    
        fake_prior = Receipt.build("synthetic-prior/1", {"cumulative_host_seconds": 12.0})
        monkeypatch.setattr(subject, "prior", lambda: fake_prior)
        monkeypatch.setattr(subject, "sources", fixture_sources)
        monkeypatch.setattr(subject, "evaluate", fake_evaluate)
        monkeypatch.setattr(subject, "train", fake_train)
        monkeypatch.setattr(ctx.sr, "revision", lambda: "3" * 40)
        cap = tmp_path / "capacity.json"
>       capacity = subject.run("capacity", cap, tmp_path / "capacity-arrays", "3" * 40)
                   ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def test_capacity_controller_to_typed_receipt_uses_real_q1_constructors_without_solving(
        tmp_path, monkeypatch,
    ):
        """Synthetic trial answers isolate controller plumbing, not scientific acquisition."""
        ctx = subject.context()
        historical = subject.load("query_tests_historical_verifier", HERE / "verify.py")
        parent_path = (HERE / "results" / "heads-continuation-development.json").resolve()
        original_verify = ctx.owner.verify
    
        def verify_archived_parent(path):
            return (historical.verify(path) if Path(path).resolve() == parent_path
                    else original_verify(path))
    
        # This synthetic controller test uses a verified historical prerequisite;
        # the production runtime's current-source prerequisite remains unchanged.
        monkeypatch.setattr(ctx.owner, "verify", verify_archived_parent)
        # Source archives need not contain Git. This synthetic instrument test
        # supplies its own revision while production execution keeps its guard.
        monkeypatch.setattr(ctx.sr, "revision", lambda: "a" * 40)
        original_factory = ctx.admission.make_brain
        original_snapshot = ctx.relevance.parameter_snapshot
    
        def no_phase(*args, **kwargs):
            pytest.fail("constructor-only controller smoke attempted a neural phase")
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(ctx.admission.heads.Graph, "equilibrate", no_phase)
    
        def synthetic_trial(arm, seed, profile, deadline, mode="agreement"):
            brain = ctx.admission.make_brain(arm, seed, profile, mode)
            assert brain.query_initialization["query_scale"] == 1
            if arm == "supplied_reads":
                assert brain.query_initialization["applied"]
    
            def panel(name):
                rows = ctx.task.panel(name)
                answer = ctx.task.targets(rows)
                # Capacity must report held-out failure without adding a hidden
                # held-out accuracy floor to the original trained-content gate.
                mask = np.array([tuple(row[:2]) in ctx.task.HELD_PAIRS for row in rows])
                answer[mask] = (answer[mask] + 1) % 4
                return ctx.relevance.reading(answer, rows)
    
            initial = ctx.sr.parameter_digest(brain)
            schedule = ctx.task.draw(seed, 512, 16)
            return {"arm": arm, "seed": seed, "mode": mode,
                "training": training(512), "initial": panel("all"),
                "evaluation": {name: panel(name) for name in ("train", "held_out", "all")},
                "initial_parameters": initial, "final_parameters": initial,
                "evaluation_parameters": initial, "parameters_preserved": True,
                "resolved": brain.describe(), "neuron_model": brain.brain.neuron_model.to_dict(),
                "numerical_policy": ctx.admission.numerical_settings(brain),
                "learning_config": brain.learner.config.to_dict(),
                "neurons": brain.connectome.n, "synapses": brain.connectome.synapses,
                "schedule_sha256": canonical_sha256(schedule),
                "work": {name: 0 for name in ("cortical_synapse_transports", "memory_scan_macs",
                    "softmax_elements", "memory_drive_evaluations", "fixed_read_coordinate_updates")},
                "elapsed_host_seconds": 0., "acquired": True}
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(ctx.admission, "trial", synthetic_trial)
        path = tmp_path / "capacity.json"
>       receipt = subject.run("capacity", path, tmp_path / "parameters", ctx.sr.revision())
